tbmm-2015-03-31sit01spe0002par0015	Mr. President, honorable lawmakers, I salute you all. Influenza viruses can replicate in the epithelial cells of the respiratory system, causing tracheitis, bronchitis, bronchiolitis, lung edema, common alveol damage and bleeding. Clinical symptoms include sudden fever, cough, fatigue, muscle aches and headaches. The disease spreads rapidly, causing epidemics. He often recovers on his own, but sometimes he looks slow and ends up dead. Around the world, 20 percent of adults are infected every year, 5% of children are infected. Because of the influenza infection, between 250,000 and 500,000 people are lost a year. In World War I, 20 million people were lost to the Spanish flu pandemic. The Influenza infection has been monitored by the World Health Organization since 1952 because of its threat to public health. Turkey began this surveillance in 2005. The first stage of infection is monitored in health care, and the properties of the virus, which is a factor in that season, are considered to be the compatibility and antiviral resistance of the vaccine with the virus in circulation. Examples of patients are being examined by the National Influenza Centre for Public Health Foundation of Turkey, İstanbul Medical Faculty National Influenza Reference Laboratory and Istanbul Public Health Laboratory. Influenza infection in our country is also assessed by the hospital-based global surveillance network project, starting in 2013. Influenza A H1N1, which humans had never encountered in the flu season of 2009-2010, quickly spread throughout the world. This is called swine flu because it's genetically similar to the pig influenza virus. In a few years, the disease has lost its pandemic properties because it infects a large number of people. This year's results and our number of patients show nothing unusual. You can't predict the flu epidemic. Due to global warming, the flu season has shifted from November-December to January, and its deadline is at the end of April. In 2015, as of the 12th week, the three reference labs received 5,240 samples. In 1,834 of these, the virus has been identified. 498 influenza A H1N1, 549 influenza B, 128 influenza A H3N2 and 659 other respiratory viruses. In 312 of the 921 samples worked on March 16-22, flu virus was determined, while in 20 other respiratory viruses were detected. As of this morning's flu season, 54 patients who have been confirmed with the Influenza virus have been sadly lost. The average age of patients is 55. The youngest patient lost is 5 years old. 17 patients, 65 and more years old. One of the seven patients lost in Samsun is pregnant. Influenza A H1N1, 5 influenza B, 7 influenza H3N2. 34 of our patients have other diseases with them. I'm sorry for our lost patients, my condolences to their families. As of March 2015 in Europe, flu activity continues to be moderate in some countries, with a tendency to decline in some countries. Influenza A H3N2 is still dominant at the beginning of the season, while influenza B appears to have taken the lead. 369 of the patients admitted in Europe due to influenza infections have been lost. The majority of lost patients are over 65 years old, and more than half of the Influenza A H3N2 sushi has differed from the sushi in the vaccine. That would increase the number of deaths. In the United States of America, 96.680 influenza positives have been determined in five months, 96 percent of the Influenza A phenomenon is H3N2. In China and neighbouring countries, 517 people have been identified with H7N9 water in birds. Due to the genetic mutation of the H7N9 virus, there is a high risk of a pandemic factor in the future. The impact of the influenza vaccine on the frequency of infection, the rate of hospitalization and mortality should be discussed. The influenza virus in the vaccine that will be used and the incompatibility that spread during that season reduces the protective power of the vaccine. Pregnants have been told that the vaccine can be useful in reducing influenza-related complications. Personal hygiene and a healthy lifestyle are of great importance in dealing with illness. The development of influenza infections, pre-infection diseases, pneumonia, acute respiratory dissections, and other bacterial infections has had a negative effect. Smoking, alcohol, and sediter life have increased the risk of death by severalfold influenza infections. I wish you all the best, with all due respect, that we look forward to victory, to the scientists who worked in the labs to turn the annual wars of humanity's influenza viruses into permanent peace.

Our mentally challenged citizens have great problems in taking their rightful place in the community and getting education, accessing education, health care, doctoring, or social activities, and transportation is at the top of those problems. Our mentally disabled citizens are either more than 90 percent disabled. Since our mentally disabled citizen is mentally disabled, his need for transportation is provided by family members or someone who is responsible for caring for him. Therefore, it is imperative that the family member or person responsible for caring for him, such as a normal car policy, for example an automatic-speed car clinic, or the custom consumption tax of a normal-speed car economy, must be excluded. THE PRODUCTION OF HIGH SCHOOL PRODUCTIONS TO CHANGE THE LAW The following article has been added to the Law on Higher Education Institutions No. 2809, dated 1-28/3/1983. "Turkey International University of Islam, Science and Technology EC MADDE 158- A university is founded in Istanbul under the name of the International University of Islam, Science and Technology. This university is based on the presidency; A) From the Faculty of Social and Humanities, B) From Law School, C) From the Faculty of Political Information, From the Faculty of Islamic Sciences, D) From Medical School, E) From the Faculty of Engineering and Architecture, F) Computer and I.T. G) From the Institute of Social Sciences, From the Institute of Sciences, H) From the Institute of Health Sciences, It happens. For five years, the president is appointed by the President of the three candidates with academic titles as professors to be recommended by the Higher Education Board. The university board is formed by the admission of senior executives of the institutions and institutions below to give it an opinion on the activities of the university and its strategic plan. A) Ministry of National Education. B) State Department. C) President of the Board of Higher Education. The Head of Religious Affairs. D) President of the Turkish and Relative Community abroad. e) President of Turkey's Scientific and Technological Research Foundation. F) President of the Turkish Co-operation and Co-ordination Agency. G) is the head of the European Union's Centre for Education and Youth Programmes. đ) The General Directorate of Higher Education Loans and Homes. This board meets at least once a year on the call of the President. The counselling board meetings can be called by representatives without the right to vote from international institutions' higher education units and higher education institutions abroad. The work and basis of the advisory council are determined by regulations. The University Board of Directors is in charge of opening a degree and a degree programme with the department, majors, science or research centres, to determine the quotas of these programmes, and to promote academic staffs, as the minimum criteria for the quality of education established by the Board of Higher Education are met. The university puts students in their units, which are considered a scholarship from abroad by the President of the Turkish and Relative Communitys abroad. The university can make protocols with these universities in order to ensure that these students are placed in other universities if necessary. The university can make protocols and agreements on any co-operation with higher education institutions abroad, including opening programmes in languages outside of Turkish, joint programme management with foreign higher education institutions. The university Governing Body's proposal and the Council of Ministers' decision can establish universities, institutes of higher education and so on in the country and abroad. The ability to train abroad is determined by the University Board of Directors on the appropriate perspective of the Ministry of Finance and other procedures, as well as the number of administrative staff, the number of administrative staff, and the amount of fees that will be provided from their seats under contract. Within a year from the time this Law is in effect, the Foundation's establishment, the official bond of the Foundation, and the procedures relating to its registration under the Turkish Civil Code shall be established by the General Directorate of Foundations and shall be concluded by Turkey's Foundation for Enhancing International University of Islam, Science and Technology. The foundation's main goal is to support the International University of Islam, Science and Technology. The bodies of the foundation are the Trusted Committee, the Governing Body and the Governing Body. The Finance Minister is the founder of five associations or foundations set up by the Council of Ministers of the Fund for Tax exemptions and associations that operate in the public interest of the Minister of Finance, Foreign Minister and Minister of National Education and middle, upper secondary and higher education students with scholarships and housing grants. The person who joined the foundation and its representatives are the trustees of the Foundation. A) Prices for services provided by the Foundation. B) All kinds of help and forgiveness. C) The income of the property of the foundation. ) Other incomes. a) Tax on institutions (except economic enterprises) B) Due to the donations and benefits to be made, C) From stamp tax and tuition for all kinds of treatments and procedures, He's exempt. Funds and donations to the foundation can be deducted from income and institutional tax. The foundation is used by the Council of Ministers to provide tax-exempt foundations with the exceptions of tax and tuition, which are known by other laws. At the request of the Foundation, the Ministry of Finance may grant or allow for up to 49 years of independent and continuous non-profit rights for the Foundation to support the goals of the institution, including the government's sovereign property and subsistence use. Within a month from the time this clause is in effect, the Foundation's organisation will be used and the rest will be transferred from the reserve budget of the Finance Ministry budget to the General Directorate of Turkish Liras, which will be transferred to the Fund after the foundation is completed. Turkey's International Foundation for Enhancing Islamic, Science and Technology can open pre-school education, elementary school, secondary school, high school-level private education schools. The opening of these schools, conducting educational activities, auditing and other issues are determined jointly by the Ministry of National Education and Turkey's Foundation for Enhancing International University of Islam, Science and Technology." I'll tell you right now, as a lawyer, today, there are 87,000 lawyers in Turkey, and now there are 45,000 students in law schools. One of the main reasons why the level of judgment is so low is the decline of the quality of law schooling. So the lawmakers who are raised today in the major law schools of Turkey are turning to free law. Come on, they got a good education from the faculty. We call it. This time, the colleges, which have poor education quality, also rule. And once he's a judge, it's not going away again. The judge needs legal knowledge. So, for once, I'm strongly opposed to opening this law school, I'll tell you that. Second, you are now forming a university advisory board here that is not in other universities. Why are you making it up? Sir, it's an international university. It's not international if you call it international. If the university is based on an international agreement, then the university council can be formed. This is a public university as we know it. It's clear how to build a public university. Now, in the past, after the Constitutional Court stressed that universities are the highest-ranking scientific institutions and the driving force of society, the Constitution declared that scientific autonomy involved in the Constitution would require all the work and management of the freedom environment that science requires from the institution to function, and that the state's right to control and oversight over universities would not be understood in the form of managing its work. My dear friends, this advisory board here is also an arrangement that will neutralize the control of the SEC. On the other hand, it violates the principle of equality in Articles 130, 131 and 10 of the Constitution. So there's no point in bringing in an advisory board that doesn't exist in other public universities. I call again from this bench, and I summon all the young people: the Justice and Development Party has brought you, supposedly, the Constitutional Proposal for your right to be elected at the age of 18, and now I ask the General Assembly to bring him in and make that Constitutional Amendment; that's one.
